		document.removeEventListener('scroll', loadChat);

		s.src = server + '/' + psID + '/ps.js';
		if(isTimerRequired) {

				document.getElementsByTagName('head')[0].appendChild(s);
			}, timeoutInSeconds*1000);

			document.getElementsByTagName('head')[0].appendChild(s);
		}
	}
